\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)z\right\rangle\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz)+(2C_{0}+1)\int_{0}^{t}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H}|^{2}ds.

(34)

First, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N,H}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H}\right|^{p/2}\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}^{H}}\right]\leq C(p)\Bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p}(s-\underline{s}^{H})^{p/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i}\right|^{p/2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i}\right|^{p/2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}^{H}}\bigg]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C(p)\Bigg(\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p}(s-\underline{s}^{H})^{p/2}+\Big(\dfrac{L}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Big)^{p/2}\left(\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{2}(s-\underline{s}^{H})\right)^{p/4}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\dfrac{L}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Big)^{p/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{p/2}\nu(dz)(s-\underline{s}^{H})\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C(p)\left(C_{0}^{p/2}\Delta^{p/2}+L^{p/2}C_{0}^{p/4}+\Big(\dfrac{L}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Big)^{p/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{p/2}\nu(dz)\right).

(35)

Second, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\tau}^{i,N,H}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{i,N,H}\right|^{p/2}\right]\leq 3^{p/2-1}\Bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\left|b_{H}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}(\tau-\underline{\tau}^{H})^{p/2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\left|W_{\tau}^{i}-W_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{i}\right|^{p/2}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{i,N,H},\mu_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}}\right)\right|^{p/2}\left|Z_{\tau}^{i}-Z_{\underline{\tau}^{H}}^{i}\right|^{p/2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq C_{p}\left(C_{0}^{p/2}\Delta^{p/2}+\Big(\dfrac{L}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Big)^{p/2}\mathbb{E}\left[(\tau-\underline{\tau}^{H})^{p/4}\right]+\Big(\dfrac{L}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Big)^{p/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{p/2}\nu(dz)\mathbb{E}\left[\tau-\underline{\tau}^{H}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq C_{p}\left(C_{0}^{p/2}\Delta^{p/2}+L^{p/2}+\Big(\dfrac{L}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Big)^{p/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{p/2}\nu(dz)\right).

(36)

Therefore, from (
34
), (
35
), (
36
), (
27
), the estimate
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N,H}},\delta_{0})\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H}|^{2}\right]\leq\overline{C_{0}}
and the Burkholder-Davis-Gundy inequality, we get that for any
t\in(0,T]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{u\in[0,t]}|\widehat{X}_{u}^{i,N,H}|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left(p,x_{0},L,\Delta,b(0,\delta_{0}),\mu_{2},\mu_{p/2},T\right)+(2C_{0}+1)^{p/2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{0}^{t}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H}|^{2}ds\right)^{p/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}_{0}+(2C_{0}+1)^{p/2}t^{p/2-1}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}^{H}}^{i,N,H}|^{p}\right]ds

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}_{0}+(2C_{0}+1)^{p/2}t^{p/2-1}\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{u\in[0,s]}|\widehat{X}_{u}^{i,N,H}|^{p}\right]ds,

where
\widetilde{C}_{0}:=C\left(p,x_{0},L,\Delta,b(0,\delta_{0}),\mu_{2},\mu_{p/2},T\right)
with
\mu_{p/2}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{p/2}\nu(dz)
.

This, combined with Gronwall’s inequality, deduces that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N,H}|^{p}\right]\leq\widetilde{C}_{1},

(37)

where the constant
\widetilde{C}_{1}
does not depend on
H
.

Therefore, choosing
H=H_{n}
in (
37
) and letting
n\uparrow\infty
, combined with Fatou’s lemma and the fact that
\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N,H_{n}}|\to\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|
a.s. as
n\uparrow\infty
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in[0,T]}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{p}\right]\leq\widetilde{C}_{1},

which finishes the desired proof.
∎

Next, we are going to show that the moments of
\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}
depend on
t
. For this, we need to introduce the following conditions.

T4.

There exists a positive constant
L
such that
|\sigma_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|\leq|\sigma(x,\mu)|
and
|c_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|\leq|c(x,\mu)|
for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
;

T5.

For some integer
p_{0}\in[2,+\infty)
, there exist constants
\widetilde{L_{3}}>0
,
\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
,
\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}>0
,
\widetilde{\eta}\geq 0,\upsilon>0
such that for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
,

|c_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|\leq\widetilde{L_{3}}\left(1+|x|+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu,\delta_{0})\right),

(38)

and

\begin{split}&\langle x,b(x,\mu)\rangle+\dfrac{p_{0}-1}{2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|^{2}\\
&+|c_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\left(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\dfrac{1}{\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2}}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p_{0}-1}-1-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\dfrac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\right)\nu(dz)\\
&\leq\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}|x|^{2}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu,\delta_{0})+\widetilde{\eta}.\end{split}

(39)

Remark 4.4
.

Observe that (
39
) of Condition
T5
yields to

\begin{split}&\langle x,b(x,\mu)\rangle+\dfrac{p-1}{2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|^{2}\\
&+|c_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\left(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\dfrac{1}{\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2}}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p-1}-1-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\dfrac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\right)\nu(dz)\\
&\leq\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}|x|^{2}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu,\delta_{0})+\widetilde{\eta}.\end{split}

for any
p\in[2,p_{0}]
,
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

In the following, we state an estimate for
L^{2}
-norm of the approximate solution.

Lemma 4.5
.

Assume
T1
–
T5
and
A5
with
p_{0}=2
.
Then, there exists a positive constant
C=C(x_{0},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},L,\widetilde{L_{3}})
which depends neither on
\Delta
nor on
t
such that for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]\vee\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]\right)\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{l l}Ce^{{2\widetilde{\gamma}t}}&\text{ if\;\; }\widetilde{\gamma}>0,\\
C(1+t)&\text{ if \;\;}\widetilde{\gamma}=0,\\
C&\text{ if \;\;}\widetilde{\gamma}<0,\end{array}\right.

where
\widetilde{\gamma}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}
.

Proof.

Proceeding as in (
12
) by applying Itô’s formula to
e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}t}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}
, we get that for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
,

\displaystyle e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}t}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}\leq|x_{0}|^{2}+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}\Bigg(-{\widetilde{\gamma}}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}+\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\rangle+\dfrac{1}{2}\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{1}{2}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu\left(dz\right)\Bigg)ds+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})dW_{s}^{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}\left(2\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}-}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)z\right\rangle+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}-}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)z\right|^{2}\right)\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz).

(40)

First, using (
31
), we have

\displaystyle-\widetilde{\gamma}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}

\displaystyle=-\widetilde{\gamma}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-2\widetilde{\gamma}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)(s-\underline{s})+\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\widetilde{\gamma}\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)(s-\underline{s})+\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\widetilde{\gamma}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+2|\widetilde{\gamma}|\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|(s-\underline{s})

\displaystyle\quad-2\widetilde{\gamma}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+3|\widetilde{\gamma}|\left(\left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}\left(s-\underline{s}\right)^{2}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{2}+\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}|^{2}\right).

Then, using
T4
,
A5
, (
33
), and (
22
), we get

\displaystyle\max\Bigg\{\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|(s-\underline{s});\ \left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}(s-\underline{s})^{2};\ \mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right];

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\Bigg\}\leq C\Delta,

which yields that

\mathbb{E}\left[-\widetilde{\gamma}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[-\widetilde{\gamma}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[-\widetilde{\gamma}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]+C|\widetilde{\gamma}|\Delta.

(41)

Moreover, using again (
31
), we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+\left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}(s-\underline{s})

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right),b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle,

which, combined with the fact that
|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}(s-\underline{s})\leq\Delta
due to (
22
), deduces that

\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle\right]+\Delta.

(42)

Thanks to Lemma
4.3
, the stochastic integrals in (
40
) have zero expectation. Thus, using
(
40
), (
41
), (
42
) and (
39
) of
T5
with
p_{0}=2
and recall that
\widetilde{\gamma}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}t}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{2}+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[-\widetilde{\gamma}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+\dfrac{1}{2}\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\dfrac{1}{2}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu\left(dz\right)\bigg]+C\bigg)ds

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{2}+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}\bigg(-\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right]+\widetilde{\eta}+C\bigg)ds

\displaystyle=|x_{0}|^{2}+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}\left(-\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]+\widetilde{\eta}+C\right)ds

\displaystyle=|x_{0}|^{2}+2\left(\widetilde{\eta}+C\right)\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\widetilde{\gamma}s}ds,

for some positive constant
C
, where we have used the equality
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]
. This yields to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]\leq\begin{cases}\left(|x_{0}|^{2}+\dfrac{\widetilde{\eta}+C}{\widetilde{\gamma}}\right)e^{2\widetilde{\gamma}t}-\dfrac{\widetilde{\eta}+C}{\widetilde{\gamma}}&\text{ if \;\;}\widetilde{\gamma}\not=0,\\
|x_{0}|^{2}+2\left(\widetilde{\eta}+C\right)t&\text{ if \;\;}\widetilde{\gamma}=0.\end{cases}

(43)

Next, from (
31
), we have

\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}=\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}-b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)(t-\underline{t})-\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{t}^{i}-W_{\underline{t}}^{i}\right)-c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{t}^{i}-Z_{\underline{t}}^{i}\right).

This, combined with
T4
,
A5
, (
33
) and (
22
), we get that for any
p>1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4^{p-1}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})(t-\underline{t})\right|^{p}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})(W_{t}^{i}-W_{\underline{t}}^{i})\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})(Z_{t}^{i}-Z_{\underline{t}}^{i})\right|^{p}\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 4^{p-1}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]+C\Delta^{p}+C\Delta^{p/2}+C\Delta^{1\wedge p/2}\right).

(44)

Consequently, from (
43
) and (
44
) with
p=2
, the result follows.
∎

To estimate
L^{p}
-norm of the approximate solution for
p>2
, we need a series of preliminary lemmas.

Lemma 4.6
.

Let
p
be a positive even integer. For any
a,b,c\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, it holds that

\displaystyle S=

\displaystyle|a+c|^{p}-|b+c|^{p}-|a|^{p}+|b|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}{p/2\choose j}{j\choose k}2^{j-k}|c|^{j+k}\Big[2^{p-2j-1}(|a-b|^{p-2j}+|b|^{p-2j})\sum_{\ell=1}^{j-k}{j-k\choose\ell}|b|^{j-k-\ell}|a-b|^{\ell}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\ell=1}^{p-2j}{p-2j\choose\ell}|b|^{p-j-k-\ell}|a-b|^{\ell}\Big].

(45)

Proof.

We first note that

S=\Big(|a|^{2}+2\langle a,c\rangle+|c|^{2}\Big)^{p/2}-|a|^{p}-\Big(|b|^{2}+2\langle b,c\rangle+|c|^{2}\Big)^{p/2}+|b|^{p}
.
Using the binomial theorem, we have

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}{p/2\choose j}{j\choose k}2^{j-k}|c|^{2k}\Big(|a|^{p-2j}\langle a,c\rangle^{j-k}-|b|^{p-2j}\langle b,c\rangle^{j-k}\Big).

Next, we write

\displaystyle|a|^{p-2j}\langle a,c\rangle^{j-k}-|b|^{p-2j}\langle b,c\rangle^{j-k}

\displaystyle=|a|^{p-2j}\Big((\langle b,c\rangle+\langle a-b,c\rangle)^{j-k}-\langle b,c\rangle^{j-k}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big((|b|+(|a|-|b|))^{p-2j}-|b|^{p-2j}\Big)\langle b,c\rangle^{j-k}.

Using the binomial theorem, the estimates
|a|^{p-2j}\leq 2^{p-2j-1}(|a-b|^{p-2j}+|b|^{p-2j})
,
|\langle a-b,c\rangle|\leq|a-b||c|
, and
|\langle b,c\rangle|\leq|b||c|
, we obtain the desired result.
∎

Lemma 4.7
.

Assume Conditions
T1
–
T5
and
A5
.
Then, for any even integer
p\in(0,p_{0}]
, there exists a positive constant
C_{p}
such that for any
s>0
,
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
and
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
,

a)

\mathbb{E}\left[-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\leq-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}+C_{p}|\lambda|\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}
.

b)

\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\leq|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+C_{p}\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}.

c)

\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-4}|(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N})^{\mathsf{T}}\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\leq|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}+C_{p}\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-3}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}.

Proof.

First, by using
T4
, (
22
), Burkholder-Davis-Gundy’s inequality, (
33
) and
A5
, we get that for all
2\leq j\leq p
,

\displaystyle\max\bigg\{|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|s-\underline{s}\right|;\ \mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right];

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\bigg\}\leq C\sqrt{\Delta},

\displaystyle\max\bigg\{\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j};\ \mathbb{E}\left[\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right];

\displaystyle\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\bigg\}\leq C\Delta.

(46)

a) Using the binomial theorem and (
31
), we get that

\displaystyle-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}

\displaystyle=-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}-\lambda\sum_{j=1}^{p}{p\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-j}\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\right)^{j}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}+|\lambda|p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-1}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\quad+|\lambda|\sum_{j=2}^{p}{p\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-j}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j}

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}+|\lambda|p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\bigg(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|s-\underline{s}\right|+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|\bigg)+|\lambda|\sum_{j=2}^{p}3^{j-1}{p\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-j}\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}\bigg).

Using (
46
), we conclude part a) of Lemma
4.7
.

b) Using a similar computation as in part a), we obtain

\displaystyle|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}(s-\underline{s})+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\bigg\langle\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right),b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\bigg\rangle+\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-1-j}3^{j-1}|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-j}3^{j}|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j+1}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j+1}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j+1}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j+1}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j+1}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j+1}\bigg).

Using again (
46
), we conclude part b) of Lemma
4.7
.

c) Using the binomial theorem and (
31
), we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-j}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}3^{j-1}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}\right).

Then, using the estimates (
46
), we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-4}|(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N})^{\mathsf{T}}\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}+C_{p}\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-3}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}.

(47)

We conclude part c) of Lemma
4.7
.
∎

Lemma 4.8
.

Assume Conditions
T1
–
T5
and
A5
.
Then, for any even integer
p\in(0,p_{0}]
,
s>0
, and
z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}\right)-\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}\right)\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\widehat{Q}_{p-2}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|,|z|\right)+\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}C_{j,k}|z|^{j+k}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{j+k}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-2}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-2}+\sum_{\ell=2}^{j-k}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\right)+\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2j}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\bigg),

(48)

where
(C_{j,k})
are some positive constants ,
\widehat{Q}_{p-2}(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,|z|)
is a polynomial in
|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|
of degree
p-2
.

Proof.

By using Lemma
4.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}\right)-\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}{p/2\choose j}{j\choose k}2^{j-k}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{j+k}\Bigg(2^{p-2j-1}\sum_{\ell=1}^{j-k}{j-k\choose\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-2j+\ell}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{p-2j-1}\sum_{\ell=1}^{j-k}{j-k\choose\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{p-2j}{p-2j\choose\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\Bigg).

Then, using (
38
) of
T5
and (
31
), we get

\displaystyle\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}\right)-\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}{p/2\choose j}{j\choose k}2^{j-k}\left|z\right|^{j+k}(\widetilde{L_{3}})^{j+k}3^{j+k-1}\left(1+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j+k}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{j+k}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right)\Bigg(2^{p-2j-1}(j-k)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-2}

\displaystyle\times 3^{p-2j}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{p-2j+1}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{p-2j+1}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{p-2j+1}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{p-2j+1}

\displaystyle+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{p-2j+1}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{p-2j+1}\bigg)+\left(2^{p-2j-1}{j-k\choose 1}+{p-2j\choose 1}\right)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-2}

\displaystyle\times\bigg(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}||b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|(s-\underline{s})+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}{p/2\choose j}{j\choose k}2^{j-k}\left|z\right|^{j+k}(\widetilde{L_{3}})^{j+k}3^{j+k-1}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j+k}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{j+k}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(2^{p-2j-1}\sum_{\ell=2}^{j-k}{j-k\choose\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-\ell}3^{p-2j+\ell-1}\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{p-2j+\ell}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{p-2j+\ell}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{p-2j+\ell}

\displaystyle\times\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{p-2j+\ell}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{p-2j+\ell}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{p-2j+\ell}\bigg)+2^{p-2j-1}\sum_{\ell=2}^{j-k}{j-k\choose\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}3^{\ell-1}

\displaystyle\times\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{\ell}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{\ell}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{\ell}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{\ell}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{\ell}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{\ell}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2j}{p-2j\choose\ell}3^{\ell-1}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{\ell}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{\ell}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{\ell}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{\ell}

\displaystyle+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{\ell}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{\ell}\bigg)\Bigg).

By applying the estimates (
46
) and condition
T4
, we obtain the desired result. ∎

Lemma 4.9
.

Assume that Conditions
T1
–
T5
and
A5
hold, and
N\geq\left(\frac{\max\{3\widetilde{L_{3}},1\}}{2\upsilon}\right)^{2}
.
Then, for any even integer
p\in(0,p_{0}]
,
s>0
, and
z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, it holds

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}p\Bigg(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p-1}-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)-1\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\frac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right),

(49)

where

\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}:=\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j=1;j\neq i}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|
, and

\displaystyle Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right):=2\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2}\left(\Big(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{2}\right){k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Bigg(\Big(k-\dfrac{\ell}{2}-1\Big)\big(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\big)^{2k-\ell-3}\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-4}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{m=2}^{2k-\ell-2}{2k-\ell-2\choose m}\big(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\big)^{2k-\ell-2-m}\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{m}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-m}\Bigg).

Proof.

Proceeding in the same way as in (
7
), we get

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\dfrac{p}{2}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-2}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}{p/2\choose k}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2k}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{2k-2\ell}2^{\ell}\left(\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle\right)^{\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{p}{2}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-2}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{2}+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2k+\ell}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2k-\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}.

It follows from estimate (
38
) of
T5
and the estimate
\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}|
that

\displaystyle\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2k-\ell-2}\leq\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right)^{2k-\ell-2}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|\right)^{2k-\ell-2}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left((1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}})|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|+1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{j=1;j\neq i}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|\right)^{2k-\ell-2}

\displaystyle=\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\Bigg((1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}})^{2k-\ell-2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2k-\ell-2}+(2k-\ell-2)\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}})^{2k-\ell-3}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2k-\ell-3}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{m=2}^{2k-\ell-2}{2k-\ell-2\choose m}\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{m}(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}})^{2k-\ell-2-m}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2k-\ell-2-m}\Bigg).

(50)

Using the estimate
\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2k-\ell-3}\leq\frac{1}{2}\Big(\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2k-\ell-4}+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2k-\ell-2}\Big)
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2k+\ell}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2k-\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{2k-\ell-3}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}+k-\dfrac{\ell}{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\Bigg(\left(k-\dfrac{\ell}{2}-1\right)\big(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\big)^{2k-\ell-3}\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-4}

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=2}^{2k-\ell-2}{2k-\ell-2\choose m}\big(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\big)^{2k-\ell-2-m}\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{m}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-m}\Bigg).

Set
a=|z|\widetilde{L_{3}}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right).
Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{2k-\ell-3}=\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}\left(a^{2}+2a\right)^{k},

\displaystyle-\dfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{2k-\ell-3}\ell=-\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}ka\left(a^{2}+2a\right)^{k-1}.

These facts imply that

\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2k-\ell-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{2k-\ell-3}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}+k-\frac{\ell}{2})=\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}\left(a^{2}+2a\right)^{k-1}\Big(\frac{a^{2}+2a}{\sqrt{N}}+k(a^{2}+a)\Big).

Moreover, similar to the estimate (
50
), we get

\displaystyle\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\widetilde{L_{3}}^{2}\left(\Big(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\Big)^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{2}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{k\choose\ell}2^{\ell}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2k+\ell}\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2k-\ell}|z|^{2k-\ell}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}\left(a^{2}+2a\right)^{k-1}\left(\dfrac{a^{2}+2a}{\sqrt{N}}+k(a^{2}+a)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right).

Next, using
\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}{p/2\choose k}kz^{k-1}=\frac{p}{2}((1+z)^{p/2-1}-1)
and
\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}{p/2\choose k}z^{k-1}=z^{-1}((1+z)^{p/2}-1-\frac{p}{2}z)
with
z>0
, we get that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}{p/2\choose k}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2k}\left(\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{2}+2\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle\right)^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\bigg((1+a)^{p}-1-\frac{p}{2}(a^{2}+2a)\bigg)+(a^{2}+a)\frac{p}{2}\Big((1+a)^{p-2}-1\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right).

Applying the inequality

(1+a)^{p}-1-\frac{p}{2}(a^{2}+2a)\leq\left(a+1\right)^{2}\left((1+a)^{p-2}-1\right),

with
p\geq 2
and
a>0
, we get that for
\upsilon\geq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{N}}\max\{3\widetilde{L_{3}},1\}
,

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\Bigg(\dfrac{p}{2}|z|^{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\bigg((1+a)^{p}-1-\frac{p}{2}(a^{2}+2a)\bigg)+(a^{2}+a)\frac{p}{2}\Big((1+a)^{p-2}-1\Big)\Bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\Bigg(\dfrac{p}{2}|z|^{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{-3}\Big((1+a)^{p-2}-1\Big)\left(a+1\right)\Big(\dfrac{a+1}{\sqrt{N}}+\dfrac{ap}{2}\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}p\Bigg(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\Big((1+a)^{p-1}-a-1\Big)\Big(\dfrac{a+1}{p\sqrt{N}}+\dfrac{a}{2}\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}p\Bigg(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p-1}-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)-1\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\frac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right),

where we have used the fact that
a\leq|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon),\quad\dfrac{a+1}{p\sqrt{N}}+\dfrac{a}{2}\leq|z|\Big(\frac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon.

∎

Lemma 4.10
.

Assume Conditions
T1
–
T5
and
A5
.
Then, for any even integer
p\in(0,p_{0}]
, there exists a positive constant
C_{p}
such that for any
s>0
and
z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C_{p}|z|\left(\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-3}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}\right).

(51)

Proof.

By using the binomial theorem, (
31
) and (
38
) of
T5
, we have

\displaystyle|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\right)+\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}

\displaystyle=\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-j}\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\right)^{j}

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\rangle\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-j}\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|\right)^{j}

\displaystyle\leq(p-2)|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})||z||\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})||z|\sum_{j=2}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-1-j}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)(s-\underline{s}),c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right),c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}

\displaystyle\quad+|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})||z|\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-j}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j+1}

\displaystyle\leq(p-2)|z||\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\bigg(|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})||b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|(s-\underline{s})+|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})||\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)|\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|

\displaystyle\quad+|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|\bigg)+\widetilde{L_{3}}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right)|z|\sum_{j=2}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-1-j}3^{j-1}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)(s-\underline{s}),c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}+\Big\langle\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\left(Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right),c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\Big\rangle|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right)|z|

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{j=1}^{p-2}{p-2\choose j}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2-j}3^{j}\bigg(\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j+1}\left|s-\underline{s}\right|^{j+1}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j+1}\left|W_{s}^{i}-W_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j+1}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{j+1}\left|Z_{s}^{i}-Z_{\underline{s}}^{i}\right|^{j+1}\bigg).

By applying the estimates (
46
) and condition
T4
, we obtain the desired result. ∎

Proposition 4.11
.

Assume that Conditions
T1
–
T5
and
A5
hold, and
N\geq\left(\frac{\max\{3\widetilde{L_{3}},1\}}{2\upsilon}\right)^{2}
.
Then, for any positive
k\leq p_{0}/2
, there exists a positive constant
C=C(x_{0},k,\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},L,\widetilde{L_{3}},p_{0})
which depends neither on
\Delta
nor on
t
such that for any
t\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2k}\right]\vee\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}|^{2k}\right]\right)\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{l l}Ce^{{2k\widetilde{\gamma}t}}&\text{ if\;\; }\widetilde{\gamma}>0,\\
C(1+t)^{k}&\text{ if \;\;}\widetilde{\gamma}=0,\\
C&\text{ if \;\;}\widetilde{\gamma}<0,\end{array}\right.

where
\widetilde{\gamma}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}
.

Proof.

Using Hölder’s inequality, it suffices to show (
4.11
) for a positive integer
k\leq p_{0}/2
. We are going to use the induction method. First, note that (
4.11
) is valid for
k=1
due to Lemma
4.5
.

Next, assume that (
4.11
) holds for any
k\leq k_{0}\leq[p_{0}/2]-1
.
We wish to show that (
4.11
) still holds for
k=k_{0}+1
. For this, using Itô’s formula for
e^{-p\lambda t}|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{p}
with even integer
p:=2(k_{0}+1)
, we have

\displaystyle e^{-p\lambda t}\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}=|x_{0}|^{p}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\lambda s}\mathcal{R}_{s}ds+\mathcal{M}_{t},

(55)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{s}

\displaystyle=-p\lambda|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}+p|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{p}{2}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}+\dfrac{p(p-2)}{2}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-4}\left|(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N})^{\mathsf{T}}\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle\right)\nu\left(dz\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{t}

\displaystyle=p\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\lambda s}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})dW_{s}^{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}e^{-p\lambda s}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}-}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}|^{p}\right)\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz).

If follows from Lemma
4.7
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[-{p\lambda}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}+p|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+\dfrac{p}{2}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{p(p-2)}{2}|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-4}\left|(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N})^{\mathsf{T}}\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left(-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right\rangle+\dfrac{p-1}{2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}\right)+\overline{Q}_{p-2}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\right),

(56)

where
\overline{Q}_{p-2}(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|)
is a polynomial in
|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|
of degree
p-2
.

We write

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}-\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right|^{p}\right)-\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-p\left(|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle-|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})z\right\rangle\right).

(57)

Therefore, taking the conditional expectation on both sides of (
57
) and inserting (
48
), (
49
), and (
51
) into the right hand side, we obtain that for
\upsilon\geq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{N}}\max\{3\widetilde{L_{3}},1\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}+c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)z\right|^{p}-|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p}-p|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\left\langle\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)z\right\rangle\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq p\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\Bigg(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p-1}-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)-1\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\frac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)+\widehat{Q}_{p-2}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|,|z|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}C_{j,k}|z|^{j+k}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{j+k}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-2}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-2}+\sum_{\ell=2}^{j-k}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2j}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\bigg)+C_{p}|z|\left(\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-3}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}\right).

(58)

Hence, combining (
56
) and (
58
), we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{R}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}]

\displaystyle\leq p|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\Bigg(-\lambda|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\rangle+\dfrac{p-1}{2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\Bigg(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p-1}-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)-1\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\frac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\Bigg)\nu(dz)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\overline{Q}_{p-2}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\right)+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\Bigg(\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)+\widehat{Q}_{p-2}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|,|z|\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{p/2}\sum_{k=0}^{j}C_{j,k}|z|^{j+k}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{j+k}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-2}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-2}+\sum_{\ell=2}^{j-k}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{j-k-\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2j}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{p-j-k-\ell}\bigg)+C_{p}|z|\bigg(\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-2}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}+\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\sum\limits_{j=0}^{p-3}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}\bigg)\Bigg)\nu(dz).

In the following, we choose
\lambda=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}
. It follows from (
39
) of
T5
and the equality
\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{m=1}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|^{2}
that

\displaystyle-(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N})|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\langle\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\rangle+\dfrac{p-1}{2}|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left|c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\Bigg(\dfrac{|z|^{2}}{2}+\widetilde{L_{3}}^{-2}\left(\left(1+|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)\right)^{p-1}-|z|(\widetilde{L_{3}}+\upsilon)-1\right)\bigg(|z|\Big(\frac{\widetilde{L_{3}}}{2}+\upsilon\Big)+\upsilon\bigg)\Bigg)\nu(dz)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N})|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})+\widetilde{\eta}=\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\sum_{m=1,m\neq i}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|^{2}+\widetilde{\eta}.

Therefore, using the estimate
\mathcal{W}_{2}(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{m=1}^{N}|\hat{X}^{m,N}_{\underline{s}}|
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{R}_{s}]

\displaystyle\leq p\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\sum_{m=1,m\neq i}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|^{2}+\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\sum_{k=2}^{p/2}\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}{p/2\choose k}|z|^{2k-\ell}Q_{p}\left(p-2,2k-\ell,|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|,1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)\nu(dz)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+C\sum_{j=0}^{p-2}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}\right],

(59)

for some positive constant
C
.

Thanks to Lemma
4.3
,
\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{M}_{t}]=0
. Now, we are going to take the expectation for (
55
) with
\lambda=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}
, plug (
59
) into (
55
), and use the inductive assumption,
Condition
A5
and the following fact thanks to the independence between
|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}
and
\sum_{m=1,m\neq i}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|^{2}

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\sum_{m=1,m\neq i}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|^{2}\right]=\sum_{m=1,m\neq i}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|^{2}\right],\\
&\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-\ell}\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{\ell}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-\ell}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{\ell}\right],\end{split}

(60)

for any
\ell\in\{2,\ldots,p-2\}
, where recall that
\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum_{m=1;m\neq i}^{N}|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{m,N}|
. As a consequence, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)s}\left(\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{p-\ell}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(1+\widehat{U}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right)^{\ell}\right]+\sum_{j=0}^{p-2}\mathbb{E}\left[|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{j}\right]\right)ds.

Here, recall that
\widetilde{\gamma}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}
.

Case
\widetilde{\gamma}>0
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)s}\left(\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2}e^{(p-\ell)\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big)s}e^{\ell\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big)s}+\sum_{j=0}^{p-2}e^{j\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big)s}\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)s}e^{p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big)s}ds

\displaystyle=|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big(\frac{1}{N}-1\big)s}ds

\displaystyle=|x_{0}|^{p}+\dfrac{C}{-p\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big(\frac{1}{N}-1\big)}\left(e^{-p\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big(\frac{1}{N}-1\big)t}-1\right),

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}e^{p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}+\dfrac{C}{p\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big(1-\frac{1}{N}\big)}\left(e^{p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big)t}-e^{p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq Ce^{p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}\big)t}

\displaystyle=Ce^{p\widetilde{\gamma}t}.

Case
\widetilde{\gamma}=0
:
In this case, we have
\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}<\widetilde{\gamma}=0
. Using the integration by parts formula repeatedly, it can be checked that for any
\beta<0
and
q\in\mathbb{N}^{\ast}
,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta s}(1+s)^{q}ds

\displaystyle\leq C(\beta,q)\left(e^{-\beta t}(1+t)^{q}+\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\beta s}ds\right)

\displaystyle\leq C(\beta,q)\left(e^{-\beta t}(1+t)^{q}+e^{-\beta t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C(\beta,q)e^{-\beta t}(1+t)^{q},

for some constant
C(\beta,q)>0
. This deduces that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)s}\left(\sum_{\ell=2}^{p-2}\left(1+s\right)^{(p-\ell)/2}\left(1+s\right)^{\ell/2}+\sum_{j=0}^{p-2}\left(1+s\right)^{j/2}\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)s}\left(1+s\right)^{p/2}ds

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+Ce^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}\left(1+t\right)^{p/2}.

Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]\leq C\left(1+t\right)^{p/2}.

Case
\widetilde{\gamma}<0
:
In this case, we have
\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}<\widetilde{\gamma}<0
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)t}\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]

\displaystyle\leq|x_{0}|^{p}+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-p\big(\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\frac{\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}}{N}\big)s}ds,

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]\leq C.

Consequently, combining with (
44
), we conclude that (
4.11
) holds for
k=k_{0}+1
. Thus, the result follows.
∎

Remark 4.12
.

When condition
A6
and all conditions of Proposition
4.11
are satisfied, we get the following estimate on the expectation of the number of timesteps
N_{T}

\mathbb{E}\left[N_{T}-1\right]\leq\dfrac{C}{\Delta},

(61)

for any
T>0
, where the positive constant
C
does not depend on
\Delta
.

Indeed, the same argument in the proof of Lemma 2 in
[
10
]
yields that

N_{T}=\sum_{k=1}^{N_{T}}1\leq\int_{0}^{T}\frac{1}{\Delta\textbf{h}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}_{\underline{t}}^{N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})}dt+1.

Then, using (
22
), Assumption
A6
, and Remark
2.1
(i) and (iii), we get that for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
and
p_{0}\geq 2(\ell+1)
,

\frac{1}{h(\widehat{X}^{i,N}_{\underline{t}},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})}\leq C\left(1+|\widehat{X}^{i,N}_{\underline{t}}|^{p_{0}}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{p_{0}}(\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0})\right)\leq C\left(1+|\widehat{X}^{i,N}_{\underline{t}}|^{p_{0}}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{m=1}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{m,N}\right|^{p_{0}}\right).

This, combined with

\textbf{h}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}_{\underline{t}},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})=\min\{h(\widehat{X}^{1,N}_{\underline{t}},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}),\ldots,h(\widehat{X}^{N,N}_{\underline{t}},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\}
, Lemma
4.3
and Proposition
4.11
, shows the estimate (
61
).

Next, the difference between
\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}
and
\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}
has the following uniform bound in time.

Lemma 4.13
.

Let all conditions of Proposition
4.11
be satisfied. Then for any
p\in[2,p_{0}]
, there exists a positive constant
C_{p}=C(p,L)
such that

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{t}}\right]\leq C_{p}\Delta,

for any
t\geq 0
and

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\sup_{t\geq 0}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]\leq C_{p}\Delta.

Proof.

From (
31
), we have that for any
p\geq 2
,

\displaystyle|\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N}|^{p}

\displaystyle=\left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})(t-\underline{t})+\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})(W_{t}^{i}-W_{\underline{t}}^{i})+c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})(Z_{t}^{i}-Z_{\underline{t}}^{i})\right|^{p}

\displaystyle\leq 3^{p-1}\left[\left|b(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{p}(t-\underline{t})^{p}+\left|\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{p}\left|W_{t}^{i}-W_{\underline{t}}^{i}\right|^{p}+\left|c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{t}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{t}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\right|^{p}\left|Z_{t}^{i}-Z_{\underline{t}}^{i}\right|^{p}\right].

This, combined with (
46
), concludes the desired result.
∎

4.3
Convergence

We are going to state the main result of the paper. To this aim, the following additional conditions will be needed.

T6.

There exists a positive constant
L_{4}
such that

\displaystyle|\sigma(x,\mu)-\sigma_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|\leq L_{4}\Delta^{1/2}|\sigma(x,\mu)|^{2}(1+|x|),

\displaystyle|c(x,\mu)-c_{\Delta}(x,\mu)|\leq L_{4}\Delta^{1/2}|c(x,\mu)|^{2}\left(1+|x|+|b(x,\mu)|\right),

for all
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
.

Remark 4.14
.

If we choose

\displaystyle\sigma_{\Delta}(x,\mu)=\dfrac{\sigma(x,\mu)}{1+\Delta^{1/2}|\sigma(x,\mu)|(1+|x|)},\quad c_{\Delta}(x,\mu)=\dfrac{c(x,\mu)}{1+\Delta^{1/2}|c(x,\mu)|(1+|x|+|b(x,\mu)|)},

(62)

then Conditions
T3, T4
and
T6
are satisfied.

Theorem 4.15
.

Assume that Conditions
A1
,
A3–A5
,
T2–T6
hold, and
p_{0}\geq 4\ell+6
,
N\geq\left(\frac{\max\{3\widetilde{L_{3}},1\}}{2\upsilon}\right)^{2}
.
Assume further that there exists a constant
\varepsilon>0
such that
A2
holds for
\kappa_{1}=\kappa_{2}=1+\varepsilon
,
L_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
,
L_{2}\geq 0
. Then for any
T>0
, there exist positive constants
C_{T}=C(x_{0},L,L_{1},L_{2},L_{4},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},\widetilde{L_{3}},\varepsilon,T)
and
C^{\prime}_{T}=C^{\prime}(x_{0},L,L_{1},L_{2},L_{4},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},\widetilde{L_{3}},\varepsilon,T)
such that

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\sup}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C_{T}\Delta,

(63)

and for any
p\in(0,2)
,

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\sup}\left|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{p}\right]\leq\left(\dfrac{4-p}{2-p}\right)(C^{\prime}_{T}\Delta)^{p/2}.

(64)

Moreover, if
\widetilde{\gamma}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}<0
, and
L_{1}+L_{2}<0
, then, there exists a positive constant
C^{\prime\prime}=C^{\prime\prime}(x_{0},L,L_{1},L_{2},L_{4},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},\widetilde{L_{3}},\varepsilon)
, which does not depend on
T
, such that

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\underset{t\geq 0}{\sup}\ \mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C^{\prime\prime}\Delta.

(65)

Proof.

Thanks to (
16
) and (
32
), we have that for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
,

\displaystyle X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left(b(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)ds+\int_{0}^{t}\left(\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)dW_{s}^{i}
