\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}\left(c(X_{s-}^{i,N},\mu_{s-}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)z\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz).

Thanks to Itô’s formula, we obtain that for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
,
1
1

1

From here to the end of the proof, for the reader’s convenience, we will use 3 different colors to track the changes of the quantities of interested.

\displaystyle e^{-\lambda t}|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}=\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda s}\bigg(-\lambda|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}2\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right\rangle}

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}\left|\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}}\bigg)ds+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda s}\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\big(\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)dW_{s}^{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}e^{-\lambda s}\left(|X_{s-}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}+\big(c(X_{s-}^{i,N},\mu_{s-}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}-}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)z|^{2}-|X_{s-}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}|^{2}\right)\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz)

\displaystyle+{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}e^{-\lambda s}\big|\big(c(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)z\big|^{2}\nu(dz)ds.}

(66)

In the following, we will give upper bounds for each term on the right-hand side of (
66
).

First, we decompose

\displaystyle 2\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right\rangle=2\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-b\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right\rangle+S,

(67)

where

S=2\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right\rangle
.
By using Cauchy’s inequality and Condition
A4
, we obtain that for any
\varepsilon_{1}>0
,

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{1}\left|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\dfrac{1}{\varepsilon_{1}}\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{1}\left|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\left(1+\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\right)\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon_{1}\left|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\left(1+2^{2\ell-1}\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\right)+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\right)\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\varepsilon_{1}\left|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\Bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{2\ell-1}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell+2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\Bigg(2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{2\ell-1}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right).

(68)

Second, by using Cauchy’s inequality
we have that for any
\varepsilon_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle\left|\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\varepsilon_{2})\left|\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{2}}\right)\left(\left|\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}+\left|\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\right).

(69)

It follows from Remark
2.1
that

\displaystyle\left|\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\left(1+\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\right)\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\left(1+2^{\ell}\Big(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\Big)+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\right)\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}

\displaystyle+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle+(2^{\ell-1}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg).

(70)

Then, it follows from
T6
and Remark
2.1
(iii) that

\displaystyle\left|\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-\sigma_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\leq L_{4}^{2}\Delta\left|\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{4}\left(1+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{4}^{2}\Delta\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|\sigma(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\bigg)\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\Big).

(71)

Third, using Cauchy’s inequality, we obtain that for any
\varepsilon_{3}>0
,

\displaystyle\left|c(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\leq(1+\varepsilon_{3})\left|c(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\left(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{3}}\right)\bigg(\left|c\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-c\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}+\left|c\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\bigg).

(72)

Thanks to Remark
2.1
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\left|c\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-c\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\left(1+\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\right)\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\left(1+2^{\ell}\Big(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\Big)+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\right)\left(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg).

(73)

Thanks to Condition
T6
and Remark
2.1
(iii) and (i), we have

\displaystyle\left|c\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)-c_{\Delta}\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{4}^{2}\Delta\left|c\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{4}\left(1+\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|+\left|b\left(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{6L_{4}^{2}\Delta}{\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)\Big)^{2}}\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|c(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)\Big)^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+2\left(|b(0,\delta_{0})|^{2}+4L^{2}\big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\big)\left(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right)\right)\right).

(74)

Therefore, inserting all estimations (
67
) – (
74
) into (
66
), and choosing
\varepsilon_{2}=\varepsilon_{3}=\varepsilon
, we obtain that for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
and
\varepsilon_{1}>0
,

\displaystyle e^{-\lambda t}|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda s}\Bigg(-\lambda|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\varepsilon_{1}\left|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+2\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},b(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-b\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right\rangle}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}(1+\varepsilon)\left|\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}}+{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}(1+\varepsilon)\left|c(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c\left(\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right|^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\Bigg[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell+2}+2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\Bigg]}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}2\big(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big)\Bigg[\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}4L_{4}^{2}\Delta\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|\sigma(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\bigg)\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\Big)\Bigg]}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}2\left(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\Bigg[\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+\dfrac{6L_{4}^{2}\Delta}{\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)}\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|c(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)\Big)^{2}\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\times\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+2\left(|b(0,\delta_{0})|^{2}+4L^{2}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\right)\left(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right)\right)\right)\Bigg]}\Bigg)ds

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda s}\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\big(\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)dW_{s}^{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}e^{-\lambda s}\left(|X_{s-}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}+\big(c(X_{s-}^{i,N},\mu_{s-}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}-}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)z|^{2}-|X_{s-}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}|^{2}\right)\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz).

Using Condition
A2
for
\kappa_{1}=\kappa_{2}=1+\varepsilon
,
L_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
,
L_{2}\geq 0
, we obtain that for any
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
and
\varepsilon_{1}>0
,

\displaystyle e^{-\lambda t}|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda s}\Bigg\{-\lambda|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\varepsilon_{1}\left|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}\right|^{2}}+L_{1}|X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}|^{2}+L_{2}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\Bigg[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell+2}+2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\Bigg]}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}+2\big(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big)\Bigg[\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}+4L_{4}^{2}\Delta\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|\sigma(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\bigg)\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\Big)\Bigg]}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+2\left(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\Bigg[\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+\dfrac{6L_{4}^{2}\Delta}{\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)}\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|c(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)\Big)^{2}\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\times\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+2\left(|b(0,\delta_{0})|^{2}+4L^{2}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\right)\left(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right)\right)\right)\Bigg]}\Bigg\}ds

\displaystyle\quad+2\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\lambda s}\left\langle X_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N},\big(\sigma(X_{s}^{i,N},\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-\sigma_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)dW_{s}^{i}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}e^{-\lambda s}\left(|X_{s-}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}+\big(c(X_{s-}^{i,N},\mu_{s-}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}})-c_{\Delta}(\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}-}^{i,N},\mu_{\underline{s}-}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}})\big)z|^{2}-|X_{s-}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{s-}^{i,N}|^{2}\right)\widetilde{N}^{i}(ds,dz).

(75)

Now, using Lemma
4.13
and Proposition
4.11
, we have the following estimates

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\big)\right]\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{j,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C\Delta,\\
&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}^{j,N}_{\underline{s}}\right|^{2}\right]=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}^{j,N}_{\underline{s}}\right|^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}^{i,N}_{\underline{s}}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C,\\
&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}^{j,N}_{\underline{s}}\right|^{2}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}^{j,N}_{\underline{s}}\right|^{4}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}^{i,N}_{\underline{s}}\right|^{4}\right]\leq C,\end{split}

(76)

for any
i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}
and some constant
C>0
.

Using the estimate

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}^{p}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{j,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{p},

valid for any
p\geq 2
and Lemma
4.13
, we have that for
\rho\in\{\ell,2\ell\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\rho}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right]\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\rho}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{j,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\rho+2}\right]+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\rho}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{j,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta.

(77)

Next, using Lemma
4.13
, Proposition
4.11
and the fact that
p_{0}\geq 4\ell+6
, we get that

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{q}\right]\leq C\Delta,\;\;\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\right]\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq C\sqrt{\Delta}\leq C,\\
&\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\iota}\right]\leq C,\end{split}

(78)

for
q\in\{2;\ell+2;2\ell;2\ell+2\}
,
\iota\in\{\ell;2\ell;2\ell+4;2\ell+6;4\ell+6\}
, and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\kappa}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\kappa}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\kappa}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\big|\mathcal{F}_{\underline{s}}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\kappa}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta,

(79)

for
\kappa\in\{\ell;2\ell\}
and some constant
C>0
.

Furthermore, using Lemma
4.13
and Proposition
4.11
, we obtain that for
\varrho\in\{\ell,2\ell\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\varrho}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\right]\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\varrho}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{j,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\varrho}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\varrho}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{j,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\Delta.

(80)

Using Proposition
4.11
, we obtain that for
\vartheta\in\{2\ell+2;4\ell+2\}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\vartheta}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right]\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\vartheta}\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{4}\right]

\displaystyle=\dfrac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\vartheta+4}\right]+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\vartheta}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{j,N}\right|^{4}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C.

(81)

Proceeding similarly to the above, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{4\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{4\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{6}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right]\leq C.

(82)

Consequently, plugging (
76
)-(
82
) into (
75
), taking the expectation on both sides and choosing
\lambda=\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1}+L_{2}
, we get that for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-(\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1}+L_{2})t}\left|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C\Delta\int_{0}^{t}e^{-(\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1}+L_{2})s}ds.

This implies that

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\sup}\mathbb{E}\left[|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]\leq C_{T}\Delta,

(83)

for some positive constant
C_{T}=C(x_{0},L,L_{1},L_{2},L_{4},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},\widetilde{L_{3}},T)
, which shows (
63
).

Next, for any stopping time
\tau\leq T
, using again (
75
) with
t=\tau
,
\lambda=\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1}
, taking the expectation of the above inequality on both sides and using again the estimates (
76
)–(
82
) and (
83
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{-(\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1})\tau}|X_{\tau}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\tau}^{i,N}|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T}e^{-(\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1})s}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{L_{2}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\boldsymbol{X}^{N}},\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\dfrac{6}{\varepsilon_{1}}L^{2}\Bigg[\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell+2}+2^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{2\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\Bigg]}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}2\big(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big)\Bigg[\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,1}+4L_{4}^{2}\Delta\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|\sigma(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\bigg)\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}\Big)\Bigg]}

\displaystyle\quad+{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}2\left(1+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)\Bigg[\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\bigg(\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell+2}+2^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\times\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)+(2^{\ell}+1)\left|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{\ell}\left|\widehat{X}_{s}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\right|^{2}+(2^{\ell}+1)\big|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}\big|^{\ell}\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\left(\mu_{s}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}}\right)\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}+\dfrac{6L_{4}^{2}\Delta}{\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)}\bigg(16\Big(\dfrac{L\widetilde{L}}{1+\varepsilon}\Big)^{2}\Big(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\Big)\Big(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{4}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{4}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\Big)+4\left|c(0,\delta_{0})\right|^{4}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{0}^{d}}|z|^{2}\nu(dz)\Big)^{2}\bigg)}

\displaystyle\quad{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}\times\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+2\left(|b(0,\delta_{0})|^{2}+4L^{2}\left(1+|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2\ell}\right)\left(|\widehat{X}_{\underline{s}}^{i,N}|^{2}+\mathcal{W}_{2}^{2}\big(\mu_{\underline{s}}^{\widehat{\boldsymbol{X}}^{N}},\delta_{0}\big)\right)\right)\right)\Bigg]}\Bigg\}ds

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{C}_{T}\Delta,

for some constant
\widetilde{C}_{T}>0
.

Therefore, due to Proposition IV.4.7 in
[
30
]
, we get that for any
p\in(0,2)
,

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\mathbb{E}\left[\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\sup}e^{-\frac{p(\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1})t}{2}}|X_{t}^{i,N}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}|^{p}\right]\leq\left(\dfrac{2-p/2}{1-p/2}\right)(\widetilde{C}_{T}\Delta)^{p/2},

which, combined with the fact that
e^{-\frac{p(\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1})t}{2}}\geq e^{-\frac{p|\varepsilon_{1}+L_{1}|T}{2}}
, concludes (
64
).

Moreover, when
L_{1}+L_{2}<0
, we can always choose
\varepsilon_{1}>0
such that
L_{1}+L_{2}+\varepsilon_{1}<0
. Consequently, when
L_{1}+L_{2}<0
and
\widetilde{\gamma}<0
, the constant
C_{T}
in (
83
) now does not depend on
T
. Therefore, we have shown (
65
), which finishes the desired proof.
∎

We now state our main result on strong convergence in both finite and infinite time intervals of the tamed-adaptive Euler-Maruyama scheme for multidimensional McKean-Vlasov SDEs driven by Lévy processes.

Theorem 4.16
.

Assume that Conditions
A1
,
A3–A7
,
T2–T6
hold, and
p_{0}\geq 4\ell+6
,
N\geq\left(\frac{\max\{3\widetilde{L_{3}},1\}}{2\upsilon}\right)^{2}
.
Assume further that there exists a constant
\varepsilon>0
such that
A2
holds for
\kappa_{1}=\kappa_{2}=1+\varepsilon
,
L_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
,
L_{2}\geq 0
. Then for any
T>0
, there exists a positive constant
C_{T}=C(x_{0},L,L_{1},L_{2},L_{3},L_{4},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},\widetilde{L_{3}},T)
such that

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\underset{t\in[0,T]}{\sup}\mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{t}^{i}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C_{T}\left(\Delta+\varphi(N)\right),

(84)

for any
N\in\mathbb{N}
, where the constant
C_{T}>0
does not depend on
N
.

Moreover, assume that
\gamma=\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}<0
,
\widetilde{\gamma}=\widetilde{\gamma}_{1}+\widetilde{\gamma}_{2}<0
and
L_{1}+L_{2}<0
. Then, there exists a positive constant

C^{\prime\prime}=C^{\prime\prime}(x_{0},L,L_{1},L_{2},L_{3},L_{4},\widetilde{\gamma}_{1},\widetilde{\gamma}_{2},\widetilde{\eta},\widetilde{L_{3}})
which does not depend on
T
such that

\max_{i\in\{1,\ldots,N\}}\underset{t\geq 0}{\sup}\ \mathbb{E}\left[\left|X_{t}^{i}-\widehat{X}_{t}^{i,N}\right|^{2}\right]\leq C^{\prime\prime}\left(\Delta+\varphi(N)\right).

(85)

Proof.

As a consequence of Proposition
3.2
and Theorem
4.15
, the proof is straightforward. Thus, we omit it.
∎

5
Numerical experiment

In this section, we consider the rate of convergence of the tamed-adaptive Euler-Maruyama scheme (
21
),(
22
), (
62
) in Theorem
4.15
for fixed large values of
N
.
We consider the following Lévy-driven McKean-Vlasov stochastic differential equation

dX_{t}=\left(-1-3\left(X_{t}+\mathbb{E}\left[X_{t}\right]\right)-X_{t}|X_{t}|^{0.3}\right)dt+0.2\left(1+|X_{t}|^{1.1}+\mathbb{E}\left[X_{t}\right]\right)dW_{t}+0.2\left(X_{t-}+\mathbb{E}\left[X_{t-}\right]\right)dZ_{t}.

(86)

That is,

\displaystyle b(x,\mu)=-1-3\left(x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}z\mu(dz)\right)-x|x|^{0.3},

\displaystyle\sigma(x,\mu)=0.2\left(1+|x|^{1.1}+\int_{\mathbb{R}}z\mu(dz)\right),\qquad c(x,\mu)=0.2\left(x+\int_{\mathbb{R}}z\mu(dz)\right),

for all
x\in\mathbb{R}
and
\mu\in\mathcal{P}_{2}(\mathbb{R})
. Here we suppose that
Z=(Z_{t})_{t\geq 0}
is a bilateral Gamma process whose scale parameter is 5 and shape parameter is 1. It is straightforward to verify that these coefficients satisfy Conditions
A1–A7
and
T2–T6
.

In the following, we will implement the tamed-adaptive Euler approximation scheme (
21
)-(
22
) with

N=500,\ x_{0}=1,\ell=0.3,p_{0}=8
, and
T=10.

Since the exact solution of equation (
86
) is unknown, we will derive the rate of convergence of the tamed-adaptive Euler approximation scheme (
21
)-(
22
) in an indirect way as in
[
16
,
17
]
.
We consider the mean squared difference of
\widehat{X}
on two consecutive levels as follows:

\textrm{MSE}(\textbf{l},T)=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{k=1}^{M}|\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l},k)}_{T}-\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l}+1,k)}_{T}|^{2},

where for each
\textbf{l}\geq 1
,
(\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l},k)})_{1\leq k\leq M}
is a sequence of independent copies of
\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l})}
defined by equations (
21
), (
22
), and (
62
) with
\Delta=2^{-\textbf{l}}
. Here
\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l},k)}_{T}
and
\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l}+1,k)}_{T}
must be simulated to the same Brownian motions and bilateral Gamma processes (See Algorithm 1 in
[
10
]
).

It is clear that
\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l})}
converges at some rate of order
\beta\in(0,+\infty)
in
L^{2}
-norm iff
2^{\beta\textbf{l}}\|\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l}+1)}_{T}-\widehat{X}^{(\textbf{l})}_{T}\|_{L^{2}}=O(1)
,
which implies that
\log_{2}\textrm{MSE}(\textbf{l},T)=-2\beta\textbf{l}+C+o(1),

for some constant
C\in\mathbb{R}.
Thus we can use the regression method to estimate the rate
\beta
. Figure
1
shows the values of
\log_{2}MSE(\textbf{l},T)
plotted against
\textbf{l}\in\{1,2,...,6\}.
We see that
\beta\approx 0.5
.

Figure 1:
Error
\log_{2}\textrm{MSE}(\textbf{l},10)
plotted against
\textbf{l}=1,...,6
.
